FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 12 PFH 2023 Dated: 02.02.2023
regarding altering the limits of Mallenahalli village of Tarikere Taluk in
Chikkamagaluru District and constituting a new revenue village called
Doddaningenahalli, was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
12 PFH 2023 Dated: 03.02.2023 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chikkamagaluru
district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 03.02.2023 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

(6817)

LONG BOF UF YTD, Aoesaod, OY, Awewer, 509 on YA
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Mallenahalli village of Tarikere taluk in Chikkamagalur district to from a new
revenue village called as Doddaningenahalli of Tarikere Taluk and Chikkamagalur
district with the survey numbers included in column(4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 42, KAMGRARA 2018, Dated :
23-11-2018, regarding altering the limits of Annur village of Heggadadevanakote
Taluk and Mysore District and constituting a new revenue village called
CHIKKEREHADI was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette dated : 12-
10-2019 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Mysore district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 18-05-2019 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Heggadadevanakote Taluk and Mysore District to form a new revenue
village called as CHIKKEREHADI of HeggadadevanakoteTaluk and Mysore
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 737 KAMGRARA 2017
BENGALURU Dated: 22-12-2017 regarding altering the limits of Muniyuru villages
of Turuvekere Taluk in Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village
called as Vivekanandanagar sub village was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD 737 KAMGRARA 2017 dated: 25-01-2018 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Tumkuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 05 -02-
2018.

And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Vivekanandanagar villages of
Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet/ part with
in the boundary of Muniyuru revenue village and same will here in after be named as
called Vivekanandanagar Of Muniyuru village Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkuru
District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above

located is converted as habitation area( Gramatana) of Vivekanandanagar hamlet/part
of Muniyuru village in Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkur District.

The proposal specified above issued under section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964) shall come into effect from the
date of final notification.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Pilali Village of Hiriyur
Taluk, Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Surappanahatti as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chitradurga
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Hiriyur Taluk in Chitradurga District to form a new revenue village called
Surappanahatti of Hiriyuru Taluk and Chitradurga District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-501

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/152/2023-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1270152 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 15-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Hanchanal PH Village of Tikota
taluk ,Vijayapur District and to constitute a new village called Ullasa Nagar as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned
to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Hanchanal PH Village of Tikota taluk ,Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called as Ullasa Nagar of Tikota taluk ,Vijayapur District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Belaguru Village of
Hosadurga Taluk, Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Gandhi Nagara as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chitradurga
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Belaguru Village of Hosadurga Taluk in Chitradurga District to form a new
revenue village called Gandhi Nagara of Hosadurga Taluk and Chitradurga District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD/93/PFH/2023-RV Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1231525 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 22-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Eshwarahalli of Chikkamagalur
taluk Chikkamagalur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Medarahalli, Kattetimmanahalli &Vaderahalli as specified in schedule along
with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkamagalur
District, Chikkamagalur.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Eshwarahalli of Chikkamagalur taluk in Chikkamagalur District to form a
new revenue village called as Medarahalli of Chikkamagalur taluk
Chikkamagalur District along with the survey numbers included in column (4)
thereof.

Date: 16-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
[
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Nandibattalu Village of
Tarikere Taluk, Chikkamagaluru District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called Hunasebylu as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner,
Chikkamagaluru District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Nandibattalu of Tarikere Taluk, Chikkamagaluru District to form a new
revenue village called Hunasebylu Village of of Tarikere Taluk, Chikkamagaluru
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 05. GLH 2020 Dated : 19.01.2022
regarding altering the limits of Maliyuru village of T.Narasipura Taluk and Mysuru
District and constituting a new revenue village called Chennaveeregowdanakoppalu
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 152 Part IV-A dated :
20.04.2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Mysuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 03.10.2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
of T.Narasipura taluk in Mysuru District to from a new revenue village called as
Chennaveeregowdanakoppalu of T.Narasipura Taluk and Mysuru District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No: RD 1026 KAMGRARA 2017 Dated :
03-08-2018 regarding altering the limits of Ankalagi village of Vijaypur Taluk and
Vijayapura District for constituting a new revenue village called Sanjaynagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 167 GLH 2022
dated: 17-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Vijayapura district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 25-11-2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Vijayapura taluk in Vijayapura District to form a new revenue village
called Sanjaynagar of Vijayapura taluk Vijayapura district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

TABLE-1
Survey numbers specified in
Old Survey numbers column (2) which are to be Extent of areas tranferred to new
SL. which areas are included to form new village village SANJAY NAGAR
NO excluded from SANJAY NAGAR and new
ANKALAGI survey numbers assingned to

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of HUGYAM Village of
Hanuru taluk Chamarajanagar District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called NALLURU, KOODLOORU and YARAMBADIas specified in schedule
along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner,
Chamarajanagar District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village HUGYAM of Hanuru Taluk in Chamarajanagar District to form a new
revenue village called NALLURU of Hanuru Taluk Chamarajanagar District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Date: 26-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kariyala Village of hiriyur
taluk Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Onihatti and Gayithripur as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chitradurga
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Kariyala of Hiriyur taluk in Chitradurga District to form a new revenue
village called Onihatti of Hiriyur taluk Chitradurga District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 322 PFH 2022, Dated : 07-11-
2022 regarding altering the limits Kesturu village of Yelanduru Taluk and
Chamrajnagara District and constituting a new revenue village called K HOSURU
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. Part IV-A dated : 11-07-
2022inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chamrajnagara district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:24.08.2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Kesturu village of Yelanduru Taluk and Chamrajnagara District to form a
new revenue village called as K HOSURU of Yelunduru Taluk and Chamrajnagara
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.
